// Layer 3b: ATC4 code → donation criterion (TO BE FILLED)
// Values: "ok" | "defer_temp" | "defer_perm" | "check_staff" | "see_criteria:KEY"
const ATC4_CRITERIA = {};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// LOOKUP FUNCTION
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

function lookupDrug(hebrewName) {
    // Find exact or partial match in registry
    const ids = DRUG_REGISTRY[hebrewName];
    if (!ids) return null;
    
    const result = {
        hebName: hebrewName,
        engName: DRUG_NAMES_EN[hebrewName] || '',
        route: DRUG_ROUTES[hebrewName] || '',
        ingredients: ids.map(id => {
            const [name, atc5] = INGREDIENTS[id];
            const atc4 = atc5 ? atc5.substring(0, 5) : '';
            const groupName = atc4 ? (ATC4_GROUPS[atc4] || '') : '';
            const criterion = atc4 ? (ATC4_CRITERIA[atc4] || '') : '';
            return { name, atc5, atc4, groupName, criterion };
        })
    };
    return result;
}

function searchDrugs(query, maxResults = 20) {
    // Search by Hebrew or English name (partial match)
    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (q.length < 2) return [];
    
    const results = [];
    for (const heb in DRUG_REGISTRY) {
        const eng = (DRUG_NAMES_EN[heb] || '').toLowerCase();
        if (heb.includes(q) || eng.includes(q)) {
            results.push(lookupDrug(heb));
            if (results.length >= maxResults) break;
        }
    }
    return results;
}
